Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the greedy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 636. The resulting operator achieves bound O(776/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on greedy structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 636. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 776. QED.
